# Context and volatility: individual experiment phases

All phase records below are **planned trading-system checks**, not completed test results. Each parent retains its ten-part specification; named refinements isolate particular information, definitions or model/policy alternatives. They do not imply one process or model per row.

[Phase rules and model quality](../SPECIALIST_EXPERIMENT_PROGRAM.md) govern chronology, metrics, interactions, useful effects, failure decisions and future testing. The [complete upgrade paths and specific child bindings](../UPGRADE_PATHS.md) and [implementation constructions](../UPGRADE_CONSTRUCTIONS.md) bind every unit below. Every listed local case needs separate named assertions at implementation; a test bearing the component name alone is insufficient.

| Unit | Kind | Individual target |
|---|---|---|
| [C01](#c01) — Source-faithful range and session geometry | measurement-or-integrity | Range object, width in ticks/price%/prior-vol units, open location, geometry and complete/coverage flags. No eventual day type or win rate embedded. |
| [C01.RANGE_GEOMETRY](#c01-range_geometry) — Source and learned range geometry | measurement-or-integrity | Exact objects plus subsequent 15/30/60-minute range-path targets. |
| [C02](#c02) — Ordered break, sweep and reclaim state | measurement-or-integrity | Causal prefix state and duration; final path class at horizon maturity only. High-only does not equal bullish trade; eventual double-break is never known in advance. |
| [C02.ORDERED_PATH](#c02-ordered_path) — Range event order and repeated breaks | measurement-or-integrity | Next transition, first passage and remaining-session branch distribution. |
| [C03](#c03) — Jumbo path and internal-versus-extension forecast experts | predictive-specialist | Common-target distributions and branch probabilities with uncertainty, no mandatory direction/confluence. Contradictory branches can coexist; G01 combines target-matched forecasts. |
| [C03.BRANCHES](#c03-branches) — Separate Jumbo path specialists | predictive-specialist | Common 15/30/60-minute PATH/REACH/DEPART targets, with branch outcomes separate. |
| [C04](#c04) — Open location relative to prior auction structure | predictive-specialist | Open-location measurement plus target-specific transition distributions with uncertainty and insufficient-history mask. |
| [C04.OPEN_TYPE](#c04-open_type) — Opening structure and path attributes | predictive-specialist | 5/15/30-minute opening attributes plus future traversal/excursions. |
| [C05](#c05) — Adaptive clock, activity and auction-range discovery | predictive-specialist | Versioned `RangeDefinition`, realized causal completion event and formation uncertainty; downstream C01 object schema unchanged. Can return no range when minimum coverage/activity absent. |
| [C05.ACTIVITY](#c05-activity) — Activity and volatility stopping ranges | predictive-specialist | Future excursion/branch distribution after completion. |
| [C05.AUCTION](#c05-auction) — Auction and change-point ranges | predictive-specialist | Post-formation traversal/excursions and actionable location value. |
| [C05.CLOCK_SEARCH](#c05-clock_search) — Clock-range discovery | predictive-specialist | Future path/location utility after actual window completion. |
| [C06](#c06) — Conditional excursion and P-zone distribution | predictive-specialist | Noncrossing excursion quantiles/joint samples and coverage intervals; L04 converts them to candidate zones and can select width conditional on forecast. Forecast range is not a hard cap or reversal probability. |
| [C06.CONDITIONAL](#c06-conditional) — Conditional excursion and analog specialists | predictive-specialist | Upper/lower excursions at 5/15/30/60 minutes and named remainder horizons. |
| [C06.JOINT](#c06-joint) — Joint excursion, time and ordering | predictive-specialist | Joint PATH and ordered barrier labels on fixed horizons. |
| [C06.PREFIX](#c06-prefix) — Remaining distribution after observed travel | predictive-specialist | Future incremental excursions through fixed session/day boundary. |
| [C07](#c07) — Auction acceptance, rejection, balance and discovery | predictive-specialist | Continuous auction state, rule-state flags, calibrated next-state/path forecasts and uncertainty. No hard ban on using options in discovery or on reversals in trends. |
| [C07.ACCEPTANCE](#c07-acceptance) — Acceptance, rejection and discovery axes | predictive-specialist | Next acceptance/rejection/traverse transitions over 5/15/60 minutes. |
| [C08](#c08) — Value migration and multiscale auction relationships | predictive-specialist | Migration vector, balance split/merge events and forecasts of subsequent acceptance/traversal/continuation; uncertainty includes bin/anchor sensitivity. |
| [C08.MIGRATION](#c08-migration) — Value migration across anchors | predictive-specialist | Future discovery/reversion and location role over 15/60 minutes/session remainder. |
| [C09](#c09) — Historical range and seasonal volatility baseline | predictive-specialist | Seasonal range distribution/quantiles and variance proxy with units/coverage; unknown warmup mask. |
| [C09.SEASONAL](#c09-seasonal) — Session-conditioned volatility baselines | predictive-specialist | Future interval physical variance and excursion distribution. |
| [C10](#c10) — Garman–Klass measurement and forecast specialist | predictive-specialist | Measured variance in log-return², forecast target distribution, estimator quality/assumption flags. Annualization only for comparable display and explicitly declared trading intervals. |
| [C10.GK_ROBUST](#c10-gk_robust) — GK measurement versus forecast | predictive-specialist | Future physical variance on matched 5/15/60-minute/session targets. |
| [C11](#c11) — Yang–Zhang opening-jump-aware specialist | predictive-specialist | Component variances, YZ measurement and forward forecast with warmup/coverage and gap interpretation. |
| [C11.YZ_JUMP](#c11-yz_jump) — Opening-jump-aware volatility | predictive-specialist | Future session variance/tails and supported intraday horizons. |
| [C12](#c12) — Multiscale realized variance and semivariance | predictive-specialist | RV/semivariances, sampling/noise diagnostics and a lag-based future variance/excursion forecast; units log-return² and covered duration. |
| [C12.NOISE](#c12-noise) — Sampling and microstructure noise | predictive-specialist | Observed variance quality and future physical variance/excursions. |
| [C12.SEMIVARIANCE](#c12-semivariance) — Directional realized volatility | predictive-specialist | Future downside/upside variance, tails and first-passage asymmetry. |
| [C13](#c13) — HAR and multiscale forward-volatility expert | predictive-specialist | Forward variance distribution/quantiles, residual uncertainty, horizon/version and insufficient-history flag. Do not transform a variance quantile directly into a claimed level reversal probability. |
| [C13.HAR_VARIANTS](#c13-har_variants) — Multiscale forward-volatility models | predictive-specialist | Common positive future physical variance and distribution heads. |
| [C14](#c14) — Jump, discontinuity and burst-volatility expert | predictive-specialist | Measured jump proxy plus calibrated jump/tail/remaining-vol distribution; unknown when sampling is inadequate. Labels occur after burst/horizon maturity. |
| [C14.JUMP_SPLIT](#c14-jump_split) — Continuous versus discontinuous risk | predictive-specialist | Future jump occurrence/size, continuous variance and receiver tail risk. |
| [C15](#c15) — Implied-volatility, skew and term-structure forecast specialist | predictive-specialist | Common physical `PATH/variance` forecasts with surface/coverage uncertainty and no implied-certainty claim. Unquoted wings remain estimated inputs. |
| [C15.ATM](#c15-atm) — ATM implied variance bridge | predictive-specialist | 5/15/60-minute and session-remainder physical RV/excursions; future IV is a separate head. |
| [C15.CROSS_IV](#c15-cross_iv) — Cross-chain implied differences | predictive-specialist | Receiver tail/path and future cross-IV factor changes. |
| [C15.CURVATURE](#c15-curvature) — Butterfly and asymmetric curvature | predictive-specialist | Physical tail shape, future IV curvature and scenario node stability. |
| [C15.DELTA_SKEW](#c15-delta_skew) — Delta-convention skew | predictive-specialist | Future downside/upside excursion asymmetry and surface skew change. |
| [C15.EVENT](#c15-event) — Event variance concentration | predictive-specialist | Pre-event tail distribution and post-event variance/surface change. |
| [C15.INTRADAY](#c15-intraday) — Surface changes and dynamics | predictive-specialist | 1/5/15/60-minute future IV/skew/term changes and physical receiver paths. |
| [C15.LEVERAGE](#c15-leverage) — Joint price and surface shocks | predictive-specialist | Joint future spot/surface scenarios and physical tail distribution. |
| [C15.MODELFREE](#c15-modelfree) — Observed option-strip variance | predictive-specialist | Internal implied-variance proxy and future physical-volatility information. |
| [C15.SMILE](#c15-smile) — Full supported smile information | predictive-specialist | Physical tails and separately future surface factors at 5/15/60 minutes. |
| [C15.TERM](#c15-term) — Total and forward implied-variance buckets | predictive-specialist | Future interval variance, surface term changes and event-sensitive path. |
| [C15.VRP](#c15-vrp) — Forward variance gap | predictive-specialist | Future physical variance/tails and return/path conditions. |
| [C15.ZERO_DTE](#c15-zero_dte) — Settlement-remainder IV specialists | predictive-specialist | Remaining-to-settlement variance/tails and 5/15-minute supported paths. |
| [C16](#c16) — VX, volatility-complex and cross-market stress expert | predictive-specialist | Stress/curve measurements and calibrated future-path distributions with coverage/age. Daily index input remains the last legitimately published daily observation with age and expected next publication; it is not a fabricated intraday print or automatically invalid merely because unchanged. |
| [C16.FORWARD](#c16-forward) — Intraday VIX option-implied forward | predictive-specialist | VIX-forward changes and NQ/ES stress at 5/15/60 minutes. |
| [C16.JOINT_STRESS](#c16-joint_stress) — Joint volatility-complex stress | predictive-specialist | Joint stress states and receiver 5/15/60-minute tails/path. |
| [C16.VIX_SURFACE](#c16-vix_surface) — VIX-option smile and right tail | predictive-specialist | Future VIX-surface/forward variability and receiver stress. |
| [C16.VVIX](#c16-vvix) — Intraday implied volatility of volatility | predictive-specialist | Future VIX-forward variability and incremental NQ/ES tails. |
| [C16.VX_CURVE](#c16-vx_curve) — Daily VX maturity structure | predictive-specialist | Subsequent session/day stress and intraday receiver tail conditioning. |
| [C16.VX_OPTIONS](#c16-vx_options) — Distinct options on VX futures | predictive-specialist | Contract-style valuation then future volatility/receiver information. |
| [C17](#c17) — Scheduled events, announcements and publication state | predictive-specialist | Event state and conditional future volatility/path distribution; unknown schedule/actual values flagged. Event risk is a policy input; firm-specific news bans are P09 hard rules. |
| [C17.PRE_POST_EVENT](#c17-pre_post_event) — Known calendar and observed response | predictive-specialist | Pre-event tails; post-publication 1/5/15/60-minute path/volatility. |
| [C18](#c18) — Participation, control and effort-progress forecast specialists | predictive-specialist | Head-specific path distributions, applicability/uncertainty, and observed control features. G01 combines only shared-target predictions; L memory/range roles can act without a fine Response pattern. |
| [C18.CONTROL](#c18-control) — Independent participation/control experts | predictive-specialist | Continuation/reversal, future excursions and control-transition probabilities. |
| [C19](#c19) — Remaining-session movement and opportunity budget | predictive-specialist | Remaining `PATH` distribution, horizon truncation, reach/room probabilities and opportunity-cost features. Lower uncertainty/room can favor a nearer trade; it need not force abstention. |
| [C19.REMAINING_ROOM](#c19-remaining_room) — Remaining movement and competing obstacles | predictive-specialist | Remaining 5/15/60-minute or boundary path and feasible payoff distribution. |
| [C20](#c20) — Regime and distribution-shift conditioning | predictive-specialist | State probabilities/features, duration, model uncertainty and unsupported-state flag; no forced universal label vocabulary or mandatory expert shutdown on every change. |
| [C20.REGIME](#c20-regime) — Continuous state and filtered regimes | predictive-specialist | Future distribution changes and conditional expert applicability. |
| [C20.REVERSION](#c20-reversion) — Observed reversion and expansion diagnostics | predictive-specialist | Future reversion, expansion and barrier-order probabilities. |
| [C21](#c21) — Session transition and time-dependent path expert | predictive-specialist | Transition/common-path distributions, explicit formation/check horizons, censoring and clock uncertainty; not an automatic directional trade at a named time. |
| [C21.TRANSITIONS](#c21-transitions) — Session handoff and horizon experts | predictive-specialist | Next segment and fixed 15/60-minute/boundary path distributions. |
| [C22](#c22) — Context originator and applicability contract | predictive-specialist | Context-originated candidate with value inputs and confidence/applicability, or reasoned no-proposal; not an order. |
| [C22.ORIGINATION](#c22-origination) — Context-originated opportunity proposals | predictive-specialist | Action-value and missed-move/non-fill distribution at the decision boundary. |
| [C23](#c23) — Parkinson high-low variance specialist | predictive-specialist | Historical log-return variance, assumption/coverage flags and calibrated future variance/path forecast; historical efficiency claims are not predicted trading improvement. |
| [C23.PARKINSON_SCALE](#c23-parkinson_scale) — High-low variance information | predictive-specialist | Matched future variance and excursion horizons. |
| [C24](#c24) — ARCH/GARCH conditional-variance challenger | predictive-specialist | Conditional forward variance/distribution, persistence/fit diagnostics and instability/abstention flag. Excursion/reversal probabilities require a separately calibrated path model. |
| [C24.GARCH_ASYMMETRY](#c24-garch_asymmetry) — Conditional-variance and leverage challengers | predictive-specialist | Positive future conditional variance and tail distributions. |

<a id="c01"></a>
## C01 — Source-faithful range and session geometry

Parent contract: [C01](/workspace/planning/trading-model/components/CONTEXT.md:6). Further upgrade: [UP-C01](../UPGRADE_PATHS.md#up-c01). Status: **planned**. Data/sequence gate: parent data and implementation certification.

| Phase ID | Concrete work | Acceptance / evidence |
|---|---|---|
| EX-C01-P0: Definition, data and target | Implement the ten-part parent contract with its own declared inputs/definition. Binding local contract SR-C01: Give provisional formation, completed observation, publication and later revision distinct states. A valid range needs all required source intervals or an explicit partial-range variant. Store raw anchor coordinates and normalized multiples before display rounding. Binding substantive upgrade UP-C01: Create a causal range-relation graph with shared/unique formation intervals, nestedness, overlap, relative width, opening position and inter-anchor displacement. Keep every exact source clock as a baseline; compare relation features and boundary-robust variants before C05 learned discovery, with no retrospective best window. Register exact added targets/ports and applicable UPGRADE_CONSTRUCTIONS before dependent code; retain every source variant and the earlier improvement path. Target: Range object, width in ticks/price%/prior-vol units, open location, geometry and complete/coverage flags. No eventual day type or win rate embedded. Confirm the exact inputs, observation clock, units, coverage and model capabilities in the linked parent contract and this local definition. Freeze definition/variant/horizon and baseline before outcomes. | Complete numeric/data/target manifest; unsupported inputs remain gated. This is a planned implementation check. |
| EX-C01-P1: Reference, causality and faults | Local mandatory cases: W=100 gives EQ L+50 and upper1.33 H+133; first15m cannot freeze at session open; missing final bar, DST, body25 versus range25, London clock ambiguity and prior-RTH/6–9 anchor parity. Full-system local cases: Zero width; missing early high; a last forming trade arriving late; 1.33 extension versus normalized position 1.33; revised prior-RTH range. Specific upgrade cases: Cross-midnight window; shared endpoints; partial holiday range; inner range outside prior outer range; unfinished formation. Add suffix-deletion, delayed/corrected-input and restart equivalence through this unit and its consumers. | Named assertions with expected outcomes for every listed case; zero unexplained semantic/causal failures, bounded declared ambiguity and numerical tolerance. |
| EX-C01-P2: Isolated quality and challengers | Compare: Exact disclosed source geometry; corrected causal implementation versus source repaint/proxy benchmark; learned/adaptive windows belong to C05 and conditional bounds C06. Binding further upgrade and child comparisons: [UP-C01](../UPGRADE_PATHS.md#up-c01). Full-system comparison: Compare identical underlying intervals through event/bar references and each disclosed source anchor; revisions create new views without changing earlier decisions. Specific further-upgrade comparison: Each source range, individual geometry, multi-range relation basis and registered clock-phase perturbations. Primary local evidence: Reference algebra/state/coverage, numerical uncertainty and the independently scored downstream forecast role; Exact hand geometry and prefix invariance; source table replication only with clearly defined denominators. No calibrated use of reported 86.46%. Further-upgrade evidence: Exact anchor fidelity, phase stability, incremental path information, candidate redundancy and net contribution. | Exact services require reference/invariance consistency; any learned comparison uses the same target/horizon/cohort, separates information from model class, fits/tunes only inside the chronology, and reports effect intervals/learning curves. |
| EX-C01-P3: Calibration, support and generalization | Exact facts get fidelity/coverage/numerical-error reports, not learned confidence. Any downstream learned head, fitted transform or uncertainty model requires chronological OOF/calibration; report year/session/age/coverage sensitivity and effective observations. | No in-sample substitution at learned edges; report unsupported/rare cohorts rather than unconditional confidence. Deterministic correctness is not an alpha claim. |
| EX-C01-P4: Increment and interactions | Ablations/controls: Anchor, clock, width normalization and source variant comparisons; no usable range means no dependent projection. Class A, small state per registered window. Preserve source-faithful computable, corrected/earlier-upgraded and further-candidate stages. Separate added information, representation and learner capacity; apply the local geometry, latency, uncertainty and resource controls. No representative-only substitution for the other child/source variants. Also register no-component/simple-component and selected 2x2 interactions where applicable. Hard position/account/integrity constraints stay in every eligible policy comparison; relaxations are separate diagnostics. | Held-out increment under a frozen useful-effect/non-inferiority rule, or a prespecified joint interaction/invariant role; trial/search accounting complete. |
| EX-C01-P5: Decision consequences | Use: F03/09/10, M04 prior-RTH value; C02/C03 and L01–03. Zero-width/incomplete ranges abstain from divisions/projections. Further-upgrade use: Use relationships among the full range family while preserving the specific clocks and definitions that generated the ideas. Replace only this Context input/head before refitting its OOF consumers. Measure changed P-zone/role/room forecasts and resulting stops, destinations and waiting decisions. | Attribution reconciles with a full rerun; include rejected, untraded, no-touch and non-fill opportunities. Local score alone is insufficient for policy promotion. |
| EX-C01-P6: Integrated economic confirmation | Run the appropriate E0–E5 complete one-account/one-mini policy with independently reserved daily/account risk, actual required cutoff, all-in fees, latency/fill stress and all eligible days. Select before frozen outer/future confirmation. | Report paired daily net/risk/occupancy, account survival, uncertainty and objective gap. A useful component need not meet $2,000/day alone; the complete policy must be evaluated against that target. |
| EX-C01-P7: Future parity, monitoring and fallback | Freeze parameter policy, future endpoint/inference, input/definition/model versions and monitored local metrics (Reference algebra/state/coverage, numerical uncertainty and the independently scored downstream forecast role; Exact hand geometry and prefix invariance; source table replication only with clearly defined denominators. No calibrated use of reported 86.46%. Further-upgrade evidence: Exact anchor fidelity, phase stability, incremental path information, candidate redundancy and net contribution.). Test fallback, stale state, restart and rollback. Detailed R work waits for E5; unavailable data dependencies stay disabled. | Actual captured-receipt/replay and operational evidence when authorized; no future-test tuning. Mark supported/not met/inconclusive with scope. All trading-system phases remain planned here. |

<a id="c02"></a>
## C02 — Ordered break, sweep and reclaim state

Parent contract: [C02](/workspace/planning/trading-model/components/CONTEXT.md:19). Further upgrade: [UP-C02](../UPGRADE_PATHS.md#up-c02). Status: **planned**. Data/sequence gate: parent data and implementation certification.

| Phase ID | Concrete work | Acceptance / evidence |
|---|---|---|
| EX-C02-P0: Definition, data and target | Implement the ten-part parent contract with its own declared inputs/definition. Binding local contract SR-C02: Define breach, traded contact, gap crossing, return and retest separately against the frozen object version. Record unresolved tie sets when source ordering is insufficient. A final no-break label requires a complete horizon. Binding substantive upgrade UP-C02: Add duration, excursion, accepted time/volume, effort and return-depth trajectories to the exact event automaton. Compare shrunken transition tables with semi-Markov competing next-event hazards, preserving simultaneous/gap cases and causal state revisions. Register exact added targets/ports and applicable UPGRADE_CONSTRUCTIONS before dependent code; retain every source variant and the earlier improvement path. Target: Causal prefix state and duration; final path class at horizon maturity only. High-only does not equal bullish trade; eventual double-break is never known in advance. Confirm the exact inputs, observation clock, units, coverage and model capabilities in the linked parent contract and this local definition. Freeze definition/variant/horizon and baseline before outcomes. | Complete numeric/data/target manifest; unsupported inputs remain gated. This is a planned implementation check. |
| EX-C02-P1: Reference, causality and faults | Local mandatory cases: Both edges same coarse bar, extended high-only mean reversion, compressed lower break continuation, sweep before action window, reclaim then second break, no-break and horizon-end censor. Full-system local cases: Both edges inside one unresolved bar; repeated high breaks before low; gap across an edge without traded touch; state at exact horizon endpoint. Specific upgrade cases: Brief wick versus sustained acceptance; high then low versus low then high; repeated shallow recross; gap over edge. Add suffix-deletion, delayed/corrected-input and restart equivalence through this unit and its consumers. | Named assertions with expected outcomes for every listed case; zero unexplained semantic/causal failures, bounded declared ambiguity and numerical tolerance. |
| EX-C02-P2: Isolated quality and challengers | Compare: Exact rule state machine; empirical transition/competing-risk frequencies; multinomial/logistic survival, HSMM or temporal model for future transitions. Binding further upgrade and child comparisons: [UP-C02](../UPGRADE_PATHS.md#up-c02). Full-system comparison: Compare strict source transitions and registered hysteresis/tolerance variants on the same observed path and denominator. Specific further-upgrade comparison: Exact source states, empirical transitions, continuous state summaries and duration-aware hazards. Primary local evidence: Reference algebra/state/coverage, numerical uncertainty and the independently scored downstream forecast role; State transitions match ordered fixtures and source chart interpretations. Calibrated next-transition probabilities, including none, beat pooled frequency on held-out dates. Further-upgrade evidence: Next-branch/time proper loss, state fidelity, repeated-event stability and policy value. | Exact services require reference/invariance consistency; any learned comparison uses the same target/horizon/cohort, separates information from model class, fits/tunes only inside the chronology, and reports effect intervals/learning curves. |
| EX-C02-P3: Calibration, support and generalization | Exact facts get fidelity/coverage/numerical-error reports, not learned confidence. Any downstream learned head, fitted transform or uncertainty model requires chronological OOF/calibration; report year/session/age/coverage sensitivity and effective observations. | No in-sample substitution at learned edges; report unsupported/rare cohorts rather than unconditional confidence. Deterministic correctness is not an alpha claim. |
| EX-C02-P4: Increment and interactions | Ablations/controls: Unordered versus ordered, no reclaim/depth/duration, source-event delay. Missing path produces censored state, not no-break. Class A/B with bounded per-object history. Preserve source-faithful computable, corrected/earlier-upgraded and further-candidate stages. Separate added information, representation and learner capacity; apply the local geometry, latency, uncertainty and resource controls. No representative-only substitution for the other child/source variants. Also register no-component/simple-component and selected 2x2 interactions where applicable. Hard position/account/integrity constraints stay in every eligible policy comparison; relaxations are separate diagnostics. | Held-out increment under a frozen useful-effect/non-inferiority rule, or a prespecified joint interaction/invariant role; trial/search accounting complete. |
| EX-C02-P5: Decision consequences | Use: C01/F10/M08; C03 forecasts remaining transitions, L range roles and optional R sequences. Further-upgrade use: Forecast the evolving meaning of breaks and reclaims using their observed strength and duration. Replace only this Context input/head before refitting its OOF consumers. Measure changed P-zone/role/room forecasts and resulting stops, destinations and waiting decisions. | Attribution reconciles with a full rerun; include rejected, untraded, no-touch and non-fill opportunities. Local score alone is insufficient for policy promotion. |
| EX-C02-P6: Integrated economic confirmation | Run the appropriate E0–E5 complete one-account/one-mini policy with independently reserved daily/account risk, actual required cutoff, all-in fees, latency/fill stress and all eligible days. Parent contract: [C09](/workspace/planning/trading-model/components/CONTEXT.md:110). Further upgrade: [UP-C09](../UPGRADE_PATHS.md#up-c09). Status: **planned**. Data/sequence gate: parent data and implementation certification.

| Phase ID | Concrete work | Acceptance / evidence |
|---|---|---|
| EX-C09-P0: Definition, data and target | Implement the ten-part parent contract with its own declared inputs/definition. Binding local contract SR-C09: Separate true range, anchored excursion, integrated return variance and terminal-return variance. A seasonal baseline records covered duration, trading/calendar clock and prior-price scale; incomplete sessions are neither ordinary full sessions nor zero observations. Binding substantive upgrade UP-C09: Use hierarchical seasonal baselines that pool time-of-day, weekday/event and volatility-state effects with train-only shrinkage. Compare empirical quantile curves, recency weighting and simple additive distribution models; preserve a no-condition/persistence floor and separate opening jump from intraday range. Register exact added targets/ports and applicable UPGRADE_CONSTRUCTIONS before dependent code; retain every source variant and the earlier improvement path. Target: Seasonal range distribution/quantiles and variance proxy with units/coverage; unknown warmup mask. Confirm the exact inputs, observation clock, units, coverage and model capabilities in the linked parent contract and this local definition. Freeze definition/variant/horizon and baseline before outcomes. | Complete numeric/data/target manifest; unsupported inputs remain gated. This is a planned implementation check. |
| EX-C09-P1: Reference, causality and faults | Local mandatory cases: Holiday short session, overnight gap, no-price-change day, missing previous close, warmup and current-zero mean bias documented in Pine. Full-system local cases: Same range with different terminal variance; missing previous close; shortened session; unchanged valid price path; holiday incorrectly counted as a zero day. Specific upgrade cases: Rare event day; holiday duration; isolated extreme; changing volatility level; many zeros from unavailable data. Add suffix-deletion, delayed/corrected-input and restart equivalence through this unit and its consumers. | Named assertions with expected outcomes for every listed case; zero unexplained semantic/causal failures, bounded declared ambiguity and numerical tolerance. |
| EX-C09-P2: Isolated quality and challengers | Compare: Previous range/ATR; seasonal robust distribution; linear/GAM/boosted regression with same lag features. More complex model must beat this baseline. Binding further upgrade and child comparisons: [UP-C09](../UPGRADE_PATHS.md#up-c09). Full-system comparison: Compare pooled and partially pooled seasonality on the same target/cohort, retaining gap and short-session channels. Specific further-upgrade comparison: Previous/ATR, existing seasonal/EWMA, hierarchically pooled empirical distributions and simple GAM. Primary local evidence: Proper forecast loss/calibration and remaining-room utility; sample-sufficient session/asset cohorts with date-block intervals. Further-upgrade evidence: Proper range/quantile loss, tail coverage, stability across years and downstream value relative to added cost. | Exact services require reference/invariance consistency; any learned comparison uses the same target/horizon/cohort, separates information from model class, fits/tunes only inside the chronology, and reports effect intervals/learning curves. |

Deterministic correctness is not an alpha claim. |
| EX-C09-P4: Increment and interactions | Ablations/controls: Mean/median/quantile, seasonality, gap and decay. Sparse cell shrinks to asset pooled history. Class A/B minimal CPU/RAM. Separate added information, representation and learner capacity; apply the local geometry, latency, uncertainty and resource controls. No representative-only substitution for the other child/source variants. Also register no-component/simple-component and selected 2x2 interactions where applicable. <a id="c14"></a>
## C14 — Jump, discontinuity and burst-volatility expert

Parent contract: [C14](/workspace/planning/trading-model/components/CONTEXT.md:175). Further upgrade: [UP-C14](../UPGRADE_PATHS.md#up-c14). Status: **planned**. Data/sequence gate: parent data and implementation certification.

| Phase ID | Concrete work | Acceptance / evidence |
|---|---|---|
| EX-C14-P0: Definition, data and target | Implement the ten-part parent contract with its own declared inputs/definition. Binding local contract SR-C14: Record sampling, noise, finite-sample and threshold conventions for jump proxies; distinguish opening gaps, observed intraday discontinuities, clustered bursts and feed recovery. Zero truncated RV-minus-BV is a proxy outcome, not proof that no jump occurred. Binding substantive upgrade UP-C14: Build a probabilistic discontinuity/burst feature panel with signed jump size, clustering, contemporaneous flow/liquidity and decay trajectories, preserving deterministic threshold/BV comparators. Compare additive jump-augmented forecasts with a compact marked-event transition model; quality artifacts remain a separate input class. Register exact added targets/ports and applicable UPGRADE_CONSTRUCTIONS before dependent code; retain every source variant and the earlier improvement path. Target: Measured jump proxy plus calibrated jump/tail/remaining-vol distribution; unknown when sampling is inadequate. Labels occur after burst/horizon maturity. Confirm the exact inputs, observation clock, units, coverage and model capabilities in the linked parent contract and this local definition. Freeze definition/variant/horizon and baseline before outcomes. | Complete numeric/data/target manifest; unsupported inputs remain gated. This is a planned implementation check. |
| EX-C14-P1: Reference, causality and faults | Local mandatory cases: Single true price gap, spread bounce, stale-feed recovery, consecutive jumps, opening jump and scheduled release with no realized jump. Full-system local cases: Spread bounce; two adjacent jumps contaminating BV; feed reconnection; scheduled announcement with no move; missing observations through the alleged jump. Specific upgrade cases: Bid/ask bounce; clustered jumps; opening gap; return after outage; prolonged burst differs from isolated jump. Add suffix-deletion, delayed/corrected-input and restart equivalence through this unit and its consumers. | Named assertions with expected outcomes for every listed case; zero unexplained semantic/causal failures, bounded declared ambiguity and numerical tolerance. |
| EX-C14-P2: Isolated quality and challengers | Compare: Large-return threshold; logistic count/hazard and jump-augmented HAR; regime/temporal distribution model. Avoid assuming rare-jump asymptotics hold at every microsecond grid. Binding further upgrade and child comparisons: [UP-C14](../UPGRADE_PATHS.md#up-c14). Full-system comparison: Compare total variation, robust two-part continuous/jump forecasts and event-conditioned tails with natural event prevalence. Specific further-upgrade comparison: Large-return threshold, RV/BV proxy, jump-augmented HAR and conditional burst/decay model. Primary local evidence: Tail calibration/precision-recall with natural base rate, proper scores and value in risk/remaining opportunity; stress sparse-event intervals and no-go on unsupported tails. Further-upgrade evidence: Event/tail/time proper loss, decay calibration, noise robustness and incremental path/value contribution. | Exact services require reference/invariance consistency; any learned comparison uses the same target/horizon/cohort, separates information from model class, fits/tunes only inside the chronology, and reports effect intervals/learning curves. |
| EX-C14-P3: Calibration, support and generalization | Fit calibrators on separate chronological predictions of this complete upstream chain; score the stated target/horizon and natural deployment prevalence. Report effective date/episode/report counts, conditional coverage and year/session/regime/age/missingness uncertainty. | No in-sample substitution at learned edges; report unsupported/rare cohorts rather than unconditional confidence. Deterministic correctness is not an alpha claim. |
| EX-C14-P4: Increment and interactions | Ablations/controls: Remove calendar, pace, BV correction; matched event time/placebo dates. Fall back to broader uncertainty/seasonal volatility. Class A/B, bounded return buffers. Preserve source-faithful computable, corrected/earlier-upgraded and further-candidate stages. Separate added information, representation and learner capacity; apply the local geometry, latency, uncertainty and resource controls. No representative-only substitution for the other child/source variants. Also register no-component/simple-component and selected 2x2 interactions where applicable. Hard position/account/integrity constraints stay in every eligible policy comparison; relaxations are separate diagnostics. | Held-out increment under a frozen useful-effect/non-inferiority rule, or a prespecified joint interaction/invariant role; trial/search accounting complete. |
| EX-C14-P5: Decision consequences | Use: C12/C17/F06, M10; G02, C19 and P risk sensitivity. Further-upgrade use: Forecast the consequences and persistence of unusual movement rather than treating all large returns as the same event. Replace only this Context input/head before refitting its OOF consumers. Measure changed P-zone/role/room forecasts and resulting stops, destinations and waiting decisions. | Attribution reconciles with a full rerun; include rejected, untraded, no-touch and non-fill opportunities. Local score alone is insufficient for policy promotion. |
| EX-C14-P6: Integrated economic confirmation | Run the appropriate E0–E5 complete one-account/one-mini policy with independently reserved daily/account risk, actual required cutoff, all-in fees, latency/fill stress and all eligible days. Select before frozen outer/future confirmation. | Report paired daily net/risk/occupancy, account survival, uncertainty and objective gap. A useful component need not meet $2,000/day alone; the complete policy must be evaluated against that target. |
| EX-C14-P7: Future parity, monitoring and fallback | Freeze parameter policy, future endpoint/inference, input/definition/model versions and monitored local metrics (Tail calibration/precision-recall with natural base rate, proper scores and value in risk/remaining opportunity; stress sparse-event intervals and no-go on unsupported tails. Further-upgrade evidence: Event/tail/time proper loss, decay calibration, noise robustness and incremental path/value contribution.). Test fallback, stale state, restart and rollback. Detailed R work waits for E5; unavailable data dependencies stay disabled. | Actual captured-receipt/replay and operational evidence when authorized; no future-test tuning. Mark supported/not met/inconclusive with scope. All trading-system phases remain planned here. |

## C24 — ARCH/GARCH conditional-variance challenger

Parent contract: [C24](/workspace/planning/trading-model/components/CONTEXT.md:305). Further upgrade: [UP-C24](../UPGRADE_PATHS.md#up-c24). Status: **planned**. Data/sequence gate: parent data and implementation certification.

| Phase ID | Concrete work | Acceptance / evidence |
|---|---|---|
| EX-C24-P0: Definition, data and target | Implement the ten-part parent contract with its own declared inputs/definition. Binding local contract SR-C24: State the mean/residual process and distinguish predicted innovation variance, integrated conditional variance and terminal-return variance. Multi-step aggregation must respect modeled serial dependence; missing returns are not zero shocks. Record optimizer/domain failures. Binding substantive upgrade UP-C24: Compare GARCH/asymmetric recursions with a compact realized-measurement-augmented variance model and a residual combination with HAR. Register each equation, positivity/stability/innovation assumptions and chronological initialization; match inputs separately when testing model class versus added RV information, and calibrate predictive tails. Register exact added targets/ports and applicable UPGRADE_CONSTRUCTIONS before dependent code; retain every source variant and the earlier improvement path. Target: Conditional forward variance/distribution, persistence/fit diagnostics and instability/abstention flag. Excursion/reversal probabilities require a separately calibrated path model. Confirm the exact inputs, observation clock, units, coverage and model capabilities in the linked parent contract and this local definition. Freeze definition/variant/horizon and baseline before outcomes. | Complete numeric/data/target manifest; unsupported inputs remain gated. This is a planned implementation check. |
| EX-C24-P1: Reference, causality and faults | Local mandatory cases: T-C24: positive variance, zero/large residual, near-unit persistence, failed optimizer, holiday gaps, missing return and multi-step recursion. Full-system local cases: Autoregressive mean creates return covariance; holiday/no observation versus zero residual; near-unit persistence; failed fit; invalid heavy-tail moment parameter. Specific upgrade cases: Near-unit persistence; structural break; heavy tails; measurement unavailable; multi-step covariance assumptions; nonpositive numerical state. Add suffix-deletion, delayed/corrected-input and restart equivalence through this unit and its consumers. | Named assertions with expected outcomes for every listed case; zero unexplained semantic/causal failures, bounded declared ambiguity and numerical tolerance. |
| EX-C24-P2: Isolated quality and challengers | Compare: Constant/EWMA, low-order ARCH/GARCH, HAR, optional asymmetric volatility model only with registered equation/reference/complexity budget before use. Binding further upgrade and child comparisons: [UP-C24](../UPGRADE_PATHS.md#up-c24). Full-system comparison: Compare constrained ARCH/GARCH/asymmetric/tail variants with matched targets and complexity budgets against EWMA/HAR. Specific further-upgrade comparison: Constant/EWMA, ARCH/GARCH, registered asymmetric/t innovations, realized-measurement augmentation and HAR residual combination. Primary local evidence: QLIKE, distribution calibration, residual serial-dependence diagnostics and downstream value under same information/cohort. Better in-sample likelihood alone insufficient. Further-upgrade evidence: Variance and tail proper loss, parameter stability, horizon calibration, computation and incremental ensemble/net value. | Exact services require reference/invariance consistency; any learned comparison uses the same target/horizon/cohort, separates information from model class, fits/tunes only inside the chronology, and reports effect intervals/learning curves. |
| EX-C24-P3: Calibration, support and generalization | Fit calibrators on separate chronological predictions of this complete upstream chain; score the stated target/horizon and natural deployment prevalence. Report effective date/episode/report counts, conditional coverage and year/session/regime/age/missingness uncertainty. | No in-sample substitution at learned edges; report unsupported/rare cohorts rather than unconditional confidence. Deterministic correctness is not an alpha claim. |
| EX-C24-P4: Increment and interactions | Ablations/controls: ARCH versus lagged variance, innovation tails, mean model and extra predictors; unstable fit falls back to EWMA. Class B, CPU seconds–minutes per small fit, grid/repeated folds dominate. Preserve source-faithful computable, corrected/earlier-upgraded and further-candidate stages. Separate added information, representation and learner capacity; apply the local geometry, latency, uncertainty and resource controls. No representative-only substitution for the other child/source variants. Also register no-component/simple-component and selected 2x2 interactions where applicable. Hard position/account/integrity constraints stay in every eligible policy comparison; relaxations are separate diagnostics. | Held-out increment under a frozen useful-effect/non-inferiority rule, or a prespecified joint interaction/invariant role; trial/search accounting complete. |
| EX-C24-P5: Decision consequences | Use: F09/F11, C09/C12 targets and G02 common variance mixture; independent target score versus C13 HAR. Further-upgrade use: Test whether conditional dynamics and fresh realized information improve the complete volatility ensemble beyond simpler memory models. Replace only this Context input/head before refitting its OOF consumers. Measure changed P-zone/role/room forecasts and resulting stops, destinations and waiting decisions. | Attribution reconciles with a full rerun; include rejected, untraded, no-touch and non-fill opportunities. Local score alone is insufficient for policy promotion. |
